<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - 프로젝트는 성범이 주인공인 영어 이야기 오디오 앱(이야기 영어)이다. 답은 한국어 해요체와 쉬운 말로 한다. 커밋은 성범이 요청할 때만 한다(아직 아무것도 커밋하지 않음). 말해보카를 베끼지 않고, 아이콘은 Lucide만, 기존 디자인 유지, 요청하지 않은 것을 더하지 않는다. 앱 출력은 아티팩트다.
   - 커리큘럼: 초급·심화·고급 각 20화, 총 60화 패턴 구성(시즌/Part는 버림). 한 화 순서: 시작음 → 도입 → 대화 → 해설 → 예문과 말해보기(한 문장씩 5~8개, 뜻→3초 멈춤→영어) → 대화 다시 듣기 → 끝음.
   - **현재 요청:** "이제 그럼 2화부터 7화까지 대본만 작업해줘, 동시한번에 진행하지말고 하나끝나면 다음거 하고 qa하고 이런식으로 느려도되니까 순차적으로". 음성은 만들지 않는다.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - 대본 파일 형식: `## N화. 패턴`, 메타(`- 패턴:`, `- 인물:`, `- 다시 쓰는 문장: \`문장\` (1화)`), `### A. 도입`, `### B. 대화`(태그 `[flat]` 등), `### C. 해설`, `### D. 예문과 말해보기`(`해설자: 뜻` / `(멈춤 3초)` / `인물: 영어 || 뜻`, 마지막 `해설자: 이제 처음 대화를 다시 들어 볼게요.`), `### E. 대화 다시 듣기`(`인물: 영어 || 뜻`). 화 앞에 `---` 구분.
   - 화면 글자 `~`는 그대로 두고, 음성으로 보낼 문장만 `build_beginner.py`의 `SPOKEN` 맵(화면 문구 → 읽는 문구)으로 바꾼다. `check_spoken`이 plan에서 `~ / = + * # & % @ _ < > |` 남으면 중단한다. 해설자 문장은 가능하면 `~` 없이 쓰고, `~`를 쓰면 SPOKEN에 짝을 넣는다.
   - D에서 성범의 신원과 충돌하는 문장은 다른 인물 목소리로 둔다(build는 `pr[i+1][0]`로 인물을 읽어 지원). 쓸 수 있는 VOICE: 해설자, 성범, Harry, Olivia, 지수.
   - 조사: ing는 "잉"이라 `ing을`, Right는 `Right는`. 대사 앞에는 항상 이어 주는 말을 둔다. 새 단어는 전부 뜻을 풀고, 인물은 처음에 `동료 해리`처럼 소개한다. 1화에 처음 나온 표현은 "1화에서 배운 표현입니다". `Sorry, I'm busy` 뜻은 `죄송해요, 제가 바빠서요`로 통일.
   - 스크립트 쓰기: Write로 스크래치 `epN.md`를 만들고 `cat >> content/stories/course-beginner.md`, 파이썬 치환은 `assert s.count(a)==1`. Bash 인코딩은 `PYTHONIOENCODING=utf-8`.
   - QA: 별도 Agent(subagent_type general-purpose)로 "## N화"만 검토, 설계 문서의 여섯 항목(영어, 상황, 현실, 해설, 듣기, 한국어와 관계), 해설자 입말 확인, "tilde 기반 문구 요구 금지", 파일 수정 금지, `줄 → 문제 → 고칠 문장`, 없으면 "문제 없음". 비동기로 완료 알림이 온다.

   7) `해설자: fix는 고치다, 라는 뜻이고, it은 여기서도 배너를 말해요. please는 부탁할 때 붙이는 말이고요. 그래서 이 문장은 그럼 고쳐 주세요, 가 됩니다. 성범은 이렇게 답했어요.`
   8) `해설자: 1화에서 배운 표현입니다. 죄송해요, 제가 바빠서요, 라는 뜻이에요. 그러자 지수가 이렇게 말했어요.`

   진행 목록은 8단계(규칙 확인, 2~7화 각각 쓰고 QA, 앱에 넣고 게시), 현재 4단계(4화) 진행 중.

   **5화 초안 (I like / don't like ~, 해리, 감정 짜증과 장난, 주제 날씨·음식):** 얼죽아 농담.
   Harry: It's so cold today. (오늘 진짜 춥네요.) / 성범: Yes. I don't like winter. (네, 겨울 싫어요.) / Harry: But that's iced coffee. (근데 그거 아이스 커피잖아요.) / 성범: I like iced coffee. (저 아이스 커피 좋아해요.) / Harry: In this weather? (이 날씨에요?) / 성범: I'm Korean. (저 한국인이거든요.)
   해설: don't는 do not 줄임, I not like는 틀림, 얼어 죽어도 아이스 아메리카노를 마시는 한국 문화 농담. 말해보기: I like coffee / I don't like coffee / I like spicy food / I don't like mornings / I like cats / I don't like meetings / I like my job / I don't like winter. 다시 쓰는 문장은 맞는 게 없으면 억지로 넣지 않는다.

   **6화 초안 (I have / don't have ~, 올리비아, 주제 시간):**
   Olivia: Do you have time tomorrow? (내일 시간 있어요?) / 성범: Sorry, I'm busy. I don't have time. (죄송해요, 제가 바빠서 시간이 없어요.) / Olivia: Why? (왜요?) / 성범: I have a meeting at three. (세 시에 회의가 있어요.) / Olivia: With who? (누구랑요?) / 성범: With you. (올리비아 씨랑요.) / Olivia: Oh. I have a meeting. (아, 저 회의 있네요.)
   다시 쓰는 문장 `I'm busy.`(1화). 말해보기: I have a meeting / I don't have time / I have a question / I have a cold / I don't have cash / I have a cat / I have an iPhone / I don't have a car.

   **7화 초안 (I want / want to ~, 해리, 감정 스트레스, 주제 계획):**
   Harry: You look tired. (피곤해 보이네요.) / 성범: I'm tired. I want to go home. (피곤해요. 집에 가고 싶어요.) / Harry: It's ten in the morning. (지금 오전 열 시인데요?) / 성범: I want a vacation. (휴가 가고 싶어요.) / Harry: Where do you want to go? (어디 가고 싶어요?) / 성범: Home. (집이요.)
   다시 쓰는 문장 `You look tired.`, `I'm tired.`(1화). 해설: want+명사 = 갖고 싶다, want to+동사 = 하고 싶다, I want go는 틀림, go home에 to 없음. 말해보기: I want coffee / I want a vacation / I want to go home / I want to sleep / I want to eat 제육 / I want a new iPhone / I want to learn English / I don't want to work.

   **앱 단계 계획:** 음성 없는 화는 대본만 볼 수 있게 한다. `build_page.py`에서 `b1-NN.json`이 있는 화만 시간표를 넣고, 없는 화는 SCRIPTS 끝에 두어 EPS와 인덱스를 맞춘다(시간표 있는 화가 먼저 오는 순서 유지). template TOC에서 `i >= EPS.length`이면 재생 없이 패턴 이름과 대본 버튼만 보여 주고, read 화면과 `again` 시트(`SCRIPTS[g.ep - 1]`, `lineHtml`)가 대본만 있는 화에서 오류 없는지 8772에서 확인 후 게시한다.

9. Optional Next Step:
   성범의 요청("2화부터 7화까지 대본만 작업해줘 ... 하나끝나면 다음거 하고 qa하고 ... 순차적으로")을 이어간다. 4화 QA 8건을 파이썬 치환으로 반영하고 `build_beginner.py plan`으로 확인한 뒤, 5화를 위 초안으로 쓴다(이전 QA 교훈 반영: 새 단어 전부 풀이, 대사 앞 연결 말, 입말, 인물 소개). 5화 도입의 `~`는 SPOKEN에 짝을 넣는다. 파일에 붙이고 plan 확인 후 QA Agent를 띄운다. 이어서 6화, 7화를 같은 방식으로 한 화씩 진행하고, 끝나면 앱에 대본을 넣어 게시한 뒤 wrap_up 후 해요체로 보고한다. 음성 생성과 커밋은 하지 않는다.

<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - **Project:** 이야기 영어, an English story-audio app where 성범 is the hero. Beginner, intermediate and advanced courses of 20 episodes each.
   - **Ongoing rules:**
     - Reply in Korean 해요체, plain words.
     - Commit only when 성범 asks; nothing has been committed.
     - No audio generation unless asked. 1화 re-recording was explicitly postponed ("나중에").
     - Lucide icons only. Do not copy 말해보카.
     - App output is the claude.ai artifact: https://claude.ai/artifact/Q1MULrNeSTgtAXv1PQ5QtJ
   - **Current request:** "이걸로 대본 넣어봐". Build full scripts (A~E) for 1~7화 from the Korean dialogues the user pasted (originally from ChatGPT), review them, and put them in the app.
   - **Script rules from user feedback:**
     - Write natural Korean first, then translate.
     - Each line follows from the line before; the whole talk stays on one topic.
     - Realistic situations only.
     - Jokes only when the situation fits; no forced punchlines or out-of-nowhere material.
     - Do not insert lines just to reuse old sentences.
     - Do not explain Korean-only lines.
     - Narrator lines use real example sentences instead of `~` or blanks like 무엇무엇.
     - Episode titles may keep `~` (screen only).
     - Korean mixing only between 지수 and 성범.
     - Korean food and app names are written in Hangul.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Script format:**
     - Header `## N화. 패턴`, then meta lines `- 패턴:` and `- 인물:`. An optional `- 다시 쓰는 문장: \`..\` (1화), \`..\` (1화)` line goes on one line.
     - `### A. 도입`, `### B. 대화` (with tags such as [calm]), `### C. 해설`.
     - `### D. 예문과 말해보기`: `해설자: 뜻`, `(멈춤 3초)`, `인물: 영어 || 뜻`, ending with "해설자: 이제 처음 대화를 다시 들어 볼게요."
     - `### E. 대화 다시 듣기`: `인물: 영어 || 뜻`.
     - Episodes are separated by `---`.
   - **C conventions:**
     - Every character line gets a lead-in ("성범은 이렇게 답했어요.").
     - Every new English word is explained, and the explanation connects to the Korean meaning.
     - Exactly one "여기서 많이 틀리는 부분이 있어요." per episode.
     - Cross-episode references must be true.
     - C, D and E meanings must match for the same sentence.
   - **Voices:** 해설자, 성범, Harry, Olivia, 지수. A D sentence that would not fit 성범 goes to another character.
   - **Building:**
     - `build_beginner.py plan` checks the spoken text.
     - `build_page.py` builds `app/episode-01.html`. Only episodes with `audio/season/b1-NN.json` get a timeline; the others are script-only, which works because EPS indices match since 1화 comes first.
   - **Editing:** Python replacement with `assert s.count(a)==1`. Replace a whole episode block by slicing between `## N화. ` and `## N+1화. `. Use forward-slash paths in Python. Run Bash with `PYTHONIOENCODING=utf-8`.
   - **Test page:** copy into `E:\Temp\claude\12f846cde673f19c\70fbb840-1b3f-4a72-9a03-b2028bebace8\scratchpad\readsite\index.html`, prefixed with `<!doctype html><meta charset=utf-8><meta name=viewport content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><style>[hidden]{display:none!important}</style>`.
     - Server 8772 is bound to 127.0.0.1.
     - Server 8773 is bound to 0.0.0.0 for the phone at 192.168.35.169:8773.
   - **Publish:** Artifact publish with file_path `D:\project\learn-english\spikes\episode-01\app\episode-01.html` and the url above. Latest is version 59; the new 1~7 has not been published yet.
   - **QA:** a general-purpose Agent, async, told not to edit files and to report as `줄 → 문제 → 고칠 문장`.

     - I'm getting coffee uses 2화 ing; Extra shot 샷 추가; three hours long.
     - The coffee-run detail is realistic: Harry offers, and 성범 thanks him.
     - Practice: 같이 갈래요?, 커피 마실래요?, 하나 사다 줄까요?, 점심 먹을래요?, 쉬고 싶어요? style; the last one in Harry's voice.
     - Only 지수-성범 lines may mix Korean, so this episode has no Korean words besides food names.

9. Optional Next Step:
   - **Where I left off:** the user's latest message is "16화 진행해".

**Feedback content to give:**
- Basic verbs (be, have, get, take, do, go, make) are core. Building 초급 part 1 around them has merit, but there are trade-offs.
- 1~16 already exist with audio, so reordering is costly. The spec says 1~10 numbering is fixed because 4화 audio references 10화.
- Patterns already cover be (1, 8, 11) and have (6, 13).
- get, take, make and go have scattered meanings, and phrasal uses are hard for beginners.
- Better options:
  - Make 초급 17~25 or the 심화 part feature basic-verb episodes (e.g. "get 하나로 여러 말 하기").

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Voice pipeline:**
     1. `build_beginner.py plan` writes `audio/season/beginner-plan.json`, with keys like `cN-인물` → `{voice, keys, prompt}` (lines joined by `\n[pause]\n`, each prefixed with a tag).
     2. ElevenLabs `creative_generate_speech`: model eleven_v4, generations_count 1, flow `cWcb3B1JPfKhKRc6MW0m`, new-account tools `mcp__761d98d3-6168-4437-a364-2477b3b956b8__*`.
     3. `creative_transcribe_audio` with eleven_scribe_v1 and connect_from=[speech node].
     4. `creative_get_flow_run_status` returns content_url and words_download_url.
     5. Scratchpad `addurl.py N NAME mp3 words` writes urls-N.json.
     6. Scratchpad `ep_audio.py urls-N.json label` downloads files and checks that chunks match the plan keys, then updates cuts.json and words.json.
     7. `build_beginner.py build N` writes b1-NN.mp3 and .json.
     8. Copy the mp3 to `app/`, run `build_page.py`, then publish with `files` for the mp3s.
   - **Fix files:** `pick()` prefers the `인물2` file (e.g. `c11-해설자2`) when the key exists there. For a partial re-record, add a plan entry `c11-해설자2` holding only the changed keys.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Voice pipeline:**
     1. `build_beginner.py plan` writes `audio/season/beginner-plan.json` with entries `cN-인물` → {voice, keys, prompt}.
     2. ElevenLabs `creative_generate_speech` (model eleven_v4, generations_count 1, flow `cWcb3B1JPfKhKRc6MW0m`, new-account tools `mcp__761d98d3-6168-4437-a364-2477b3b956b8__*`).

     4. `creative_get_flow_run_status` returns content_url and words_download_url.
     5. Scratchpad `addurl.py N NAME mp3 words` writes urls-N.json.
     6. Scratchpad `ep_audio.py urls-N.json label` downloads the files, checks that chunks match the plan keys, and updates cuts.json and words.json.
     7. `build_beginner.py build N` writes b1-NN.mp3 and .json.
     8. Copy the mp3 into `app/`, run `build_page.py`, then publish the artifact with `files` for the mp3s.

<summary>

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Build chain:**
     1. `spikes/episode-01/build_page.py` fills `app/template.html` (`__TIMELINE__`, `__SCRIPTS__`) and writes `app/episode-01.html`.
     2. `build_pwa.py` writes `web/` (index.html, sw.js, manifest, icons, webm files, `PretendardVariable.woff2`, `lucide.min.js`; the latter two are added to CORE cache and copied).
     3. Deploy: `cd web && npx vercel deploy --prod --yes`.
     4. Artifact publish: `file_path` `D:\project\learn-english\spikes\episode-01\app\episode-01.html`. Republishing the same path keeps the URL. The woff2 and lucide files were already published via `files` with `root` D:\project\learn-english.
   - **Local preview:** launch config "story-pwa" on port 8765. Before reloading, clear the `shell-*` caches, or the service worker serves the old shell.
   - **Navigation:** stack `['home', …]` with history.pushState so Android back works; BASE = ['home','album']; overlay layers vNow and vLyrics.
   - **Inert handling:** inert is set on hidden layers and on base views when an overlay is up. Programmatic `.click()` on inert elements does nothing.
   - **Album model:** albums come from TOC with COLOR = {초급:['#1f7a4d','#5fd39a'], 심화:['#b5651d','#f0a868'], 고급:['#1d5f8a','#6cb6e8']}; `--c` and `--hi` are set on body.
   - **Saved state:** localStorage 'course-1' stores {ep, eps{done,pos}, speed, vol, mute, ko, loop}. Position is saved per episode, and the app resumes there on load.
   - **Repeat:**
     - `rep` = {start, end, title, sec} for chapter repeat, set from the chapter sheet only.
     - `S.loop` holds off/all/one, handled in the `ended` event.
     - `useBlob` skips when `a.ended`.
   - **Media session:** artwork is drawn on a canvas as a data URL; seekbackward/forward handlers are set.
   - **cast field:** `build_page.py` now parses `- 인물:` into `ep["cast"]`, with 성범 excluded.

   - **`spikes/episode-01/app/template.html`:** fully rewritten for the music-app UI.

       ```
       --t-display: 700 24px/1.25; --t-title: 700 22px/1.3; --t-head: 600 16px/1.35; --t-body: 400 16px/1.35; --t-sub: 400 14px/1.4; --t-cap: 600 12px/1.35
       ```

     - Lyrics footer: bigger padding, `.ly-keys` gap 44px, big button 68px, icons 52px.
     - Latest edit for natural narration breaks:
       ```js
       const clauses = t => esc(t).split(/(?<=[,.?!])\s+(?!(?:이)?라(?:는|고) )/).map(c => `<span class="cl">${c}</span>`).join(' ');
       // narrator line: wrap(`say ${l.kind}`, `<span class="en">${clauses(l.text)}</span>`)
       ```
       ```css
       .ln.say .en { text-wrap: balance }
       .ln .cl { display: inline-block }
       .ln.say + .ln.say .ln-go { padding-top: 2px }
       .ln.say:has(+ .ln.say) .ln-go { padding-bottom: 2px }
       .ln.say + .ln:not(.say), .ln:not(.say) + .ln.say { margin-top: 14px }
       ```

       ```bash
       # usage: stitch.sh <dir> <out.mp3>
       d=$1; out=$2; inputs=(); f=""; i=0
       for x in $(ls $d/*.mp3 | sort); do inputs+=(-i "$x"); f+="[$i:a]aresample=44100,aformat=channel_layouts=mono,apad=pad_dur=0.35[a$i];"; i=$((i+1)); done
       cat=""; for ((k=0;k<i;k++)); do cat+="[a$k]"; done
       ffmpeg -v error -y "${inputs[@]}" -filter_complex "${f}${cat}concat=n=$i:v=0:a=1,loudnorm=I=-16:TP=-1.5[o]" -map "[o]" -ar 44100 -b:a 128k "$out"
       ffprobe -v error -show_entries format=duration -of csv=p=0 "$out"
       ```

       while read n s g sig; do curl -sS -o "pX/$n.mp3" "$B/$s/$g/content.mp3?$Q$sig"; done <<'EOF'
       NN session gen sig
       EOF
       ```

He is barred from Atlanta for 10 years (except family events), must give anti-gang talks 4 times a year, and has drug tests and a gun ban.

<analysis>
**How the session started.** Another session (`local_fa0bbd24-436b-41dd-bd1e-f8d21e974503`) started this one with a narrow task: plan how to shorten the commentary in beginner episode 1. Its constraints were no commits, no branch switching and no app-file edits. The user's later explicit instructions override the app-file part.

**How the work evolved.**
- The user said "바로 도입은 하지말고 분석만 해봐", so the first pass was analysis only.
- I analyzed episode 1 timing: 219.3s total, commentary 124.7s (57%). Lines with no target expression took about 44s. Options A, B and C estimated about 3:07, 2:58 and 2:50.
- The user then redefined the whole format.
  - Drop the dialogue and the "my day" story concept.
  - Each episode becomes: intro → examples with short explanations → summary → speaking practice.
  - Use the user's interests and everyday words for variety.
  - Voices: two male and two female (남1, 남2, 여1, 여2), assigned to suit each situation. A line may be something the user says or something they hear; it does not have to centre on 성범.
- Episode order stays the same.
- Intro wording changed after a web search of other courses. The chosen form combines a fixed opening line with a preview of the lesson.
- Example selection criteria: function, place/relationship, emotion; interests are seasoning, not a category.
- The user rejected narrator lines that assume an unstated scene, then rejected the interest tails ("~처럼요") as 뜬금없는.
- Commentary is now minimal: meaning plus one point.
- "I'm am" restored in the episode 1 intro as "I'm은 I am을 줄인 말이에요."
- "I'm dying" explanation clarified.
- I showed episodes 1–5. The user then asked: write all beginner episodes, update the app, and remove the audio for now.

**What I implemented.**
- Backed up the old script.
- Wrote the new `course-beginner.md` (25 episodes).
- Modified `build_page.py` to build estimated timelines plus silent webm files.
- Modified `template.html` (npSub label) and `build_pwa.py` (copy only referenced webm files).
- Added a second preview server config to `launch.json`.
- Verified in the browser preview.
- Published the artifact (after one refusal and a merge check).
- Deployed to Vercel.

**Remaining.**
- Record the new rules in the spec doc and memory (memory rule: record changes in the spec).
- Wrap up and report to the user.
- The spec says review by another AI happens before audio is made; that is still pending.
</analysis>

<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - **Original delegated task** (from session `local_fa0bbd24-436b-41dd-bd1e-f8d21e974503`): plan how to cut the beginner commentary and draft episode 1 for 성범 to approve, then record the rule in the spec. The brief's constraints were "커밋, 브랜치 전환, 앱 파일 수정은 하지 않는다." The user's later direct request to update the app overrides the app-file part. No commits and no branch switching still apply.
   - **Don't touch** the album 2 / new content plan files; another session (`new-content-plan`) owns them.
   - **Memory rules still in force:**
     - Scripts are reviewed in text one episode at a time before any audio is made.
     - The narrator is a polite teacher.
     - No `~` or blank placeholders in narrator text; use real examples.
     - App output is the artifact https://claude.ai/artifact/Q1MULrNeSTgtAXv1PQ5QtJ, plus Vercel https://iyagi-english.vercel.app.
     - Record any change in the spec doc and search for outdated wording.
   - **The user's evolving decisions:**
     - The beginner format becomes: intro ("오늘의 표현은 …이에요 …. 여덟 가지 상황에서 들어 볼게요.") → 8 examples (an English line, then a short narrator line with the meaning and one point) → summary → speaking practice (8 items: 4 from the examples plus 4 new; Korean prompt, 3-second pause, English answer) → "수고하셨어요. 다음 화에서 만나요."
     - No dialogue and no "성범의 하루" story concept.
     - Voices: 남1, 여1, 남2, 여2, assigned to suit each situation. A line can be something the user says or hears.
     - Interests belong inside the English lines, not in the commentary.
     - No commentary that assumes an unstated scene, and no off-topic tails.
     - Keep it beginner-short.
     - Example selection criteria: at least 3 functions, at least 3 places/relationships (including one stranger), one positive and one negative emotion, 2–3 interests rotated per episode.
     - Episode order stays the same.
   - **Latest request:** "오케이 그럼 나머지 초급과정에 대해서 전부 대본 만들어서 앱에 업데이트 하고 음성은 일단 다 빼봐 그렇게 해서 앱에 업데이트해봐."

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - The app is an audio player whose script view is driven by timelines: `EPS` (durations, sections and timed lines) aligned by index with `SCRIPTS`.
   - Text-only mode works by estimating a timeline from text length and attaching a silent Opus webm made with ffmpeg anullsrc.
   - The PWA build is `build_pwa.py`, which writes `web/`; deploy with `npx vercel deploy --prod --yes`.
   - Artifact publish uses a `files` map with paths relative to the working directory. A publish to an artifact not yet viewed is refused first; check the saved live copy, then republish.
   - Listening progress in localStorage (`course-1`) is keyed by episode index. Order is unchanged at 25 beginner episodes followed by the podcast albums, so saved progress is not shifted.

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`content/stories/archive/course-beginner-v1-story.md`**: backup of the old story-based script, made with `cp -n`. The `content/stories` folder is untracked, so this backup matters.
   - **`content/stories/course-beginner.md`**: fully rewritten, 25 episodes in the new format.
     - Each episode has `## N화. title`, then `### 도입`, `### 예문`, `### 정리` and `### 말해보기`.
     - Lines look like `해설자: …`, `남1: …`, and practice answers like `남1: I'm full. || 배불러요.` with `(멈춤 3초)` between prompt and answer.
     - The header notes the 2026-11-09 restructure and points to the archive.
     - A grep found no `~` outside headings.
     - Episode 25 ends with "초급 과정을 모두 마쳤어요. 수고하셨어요."
     - Episodes 1–5 match what the user saw. Episode 2 uses the revised "I'm dying" explanation: "아 웃겨 죽겠어! die는 죽다예요. 한국어처럼 영어도 너무 웃길 때 죽겠다고 해요. 친구끼리 쓰는 말이에요."
     - Episodes 6–25 are new and have not been reviewed by the user yet.
   - **`spikes/episode-01/build_page.py`**
     - Added `import subprocess`.
     - Added WHO entries "남1", "남2", "여1", "여2".
     - Added SECTIONS entries "도입", "예문", "정리" and "말해보기".
     - Added `TEXT_KEYS`, `say_time()` (narrator: max(1, chars×0.16); English: 0.6 + words×0.32) and `text_timeline(e)`.
       - `text_timeline` splits narrator lines into sentences.
       - In practice it marks kinds as prompt, pause (speaker "나", text "직접 말해보세요", 3s) and answer (with `meaning` set to the Korean).
       - Gaps are 0.35s between lines and 0.6s between sections; the lead-in is 1.0s; duration is the end time plus 0.7.
       - It creates `app/b1-NN-text.webm` with `ffmpeg -f lavfi -i anullsrc=r=48000:cl=mono -t dur -c:a libopus -b:a 6k`, cached by a `.len` stamp file.
       - It returns `{"duration","sections","lines","file"}`.
     - The beginner loop in `main()` is now:
       ```python
       for e in parse(BEGINNER.read_text(encoding="utf-8")):
           e["kolines"] = []
           e["course"] = "초급"
           e["text"] = True
           eps.append(e)
           tl.append(text_timeline(e))
       ```
     - The old audio files `b1-NN.webm` are no longer used.
   - **`spikes/episode-01/app/template.html`**: npSub now reads `[E.course, cast(E), E.text && '음성 준비 중 · 대본만'].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')`.
   - **`spikes/episode-01/build_pwa.py`**: copies only the webm files the page references:
     ```python
     files = [f for f in sorted(APP.glob("*.webm")) if f'"{f.name}"' in body]
     ```
   - **`.claude/launch.json`**: added config "story-pwa-2" (python http.server 8771, serving `spikes/episode-01/web`). The original "story-pwa" on 8765 is in use by another chat.
   - **Build results:**
     - 25 timelines, estimated durations about 2:23–3:04 (episode 1 is 161.8s).
     - 33 audio files in `web/` (25 silent plus 8 podcast), 8,744 KB total.
     - Silent webm files are about 98 KB each.
   - **Reference only:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-07-story-audio-english-design.md`. Relevant lines: about 233–247 (episode structure and story narration), 295–327 (time table and commentary content), 343, 352 (voices) and 401–410 (commentary rules). It has not been edited yet.

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **Preview server on the wrong port.** The first preview attempt landed on port 8767 while the hardcoded runtimeArgs used 8766, so the page showed a chrome-error. Fixed by changing the config to 8771 in both the args and the port, then restarting the preview.
   - **First artifact publish refused.** The live version had not been viewed. I diffed the saved live copy (`tool-results/artifact-ba4ece1d-1794203585-94f6.html`) against the new build. The only differences were the data lines (EPS and SCRIPTS), the npSub edit and the publish skeleton. Both builds include the podcast albums r1 and r2.
   - **Files map path error.** Paths like `spikes/episode-01/app/...` were "not found" because the working directory was `spikes/episode-01`. Fixed by using `app/b1-NN-text.webm`.
   - **Second refusal.** The identical resend was refused once more; resending unchanged then succeeded (Version 103, id 1794204978-6a60).
   - **Vercel output.** The first deploy output was truncated; rerunning showed READY and the alias https://iyagi-english.vercel.app.
   - **User feedback during scripting:**
     - The listed examples in the intro were awkward.

       ```js
       const play = () => { a.play().catch(() => { if (a.paused) a.addEventListener('canplay', () => a.play().catch(() => {}), { once: true }) }) };
       let wantPlay = false, retries = 0;
       a.addEventListener('play', () => { wantPlay = true });
       a.addEventListener('pause', () => { if (!a.error) wantPlay = false });
       a.addEventListener('playing', () => { retries = 0 });
       a.addEventListener('error', () => {
         const at = S.pos, again = wantPlay;
         if (a.src.startsWith('blob:')) blobUrl = null;
         if (retries++ > 3) return;
         ready = false; S.pos = at;
         a.src = blobUrl || T.file; a.load();
         if (again) setTimeout(play, blobUrl ? 0 : 1500 * retries);
       });
       const warmed = new Set();
       function warmNext() { const n = nextIn(1); if (n === undefined || warmed.has(n)) return;
         warmed.add(n); fetch(EPS[n].file).then(r => r.blob()).catch(() => warmed.delete(n)); }
       a.addEventListener('playing', warmNext);
       ```
     - **Build output:** 33 timelines, 33 audio files, 8730 KB.

4. **Errors and fixes**
   - The cover shadow was clipped; turning off clipsContent on `현재 곡` and `상단` fixed it.
   - The grabber was left-aligned; wrapping it in a FILL row with center alignment fixed it.
   - The active tracklist icon stroke didn't apply at first; reapplying it fixed it. Then the user rejected any background shape, so the icon swaps to the lyrics icon instead.
   - **Preview:** story-pwa-2 started on port 8774 because 8771 was busy. Navigating to http://localhost:8774/ returned "navigation to http://localhost:8774 was denied or failed", and the screenshot was blank dark. Not resolved yet.

5. **Problem Solving: likely cause of background stops (not verified on the device)**
   - When an episode ends, the app moves to the next one and loads its audio from the network for the first time. The service worker must download the whole file before answering. With the screen off, Android may block the network (Doze or background data limits), so loading fails and `play()` is rejected silently.
   - A network error in the middle of playback also stopped audio for good, because the old error handler only covered blob sources.
   - "Reopens paused": once audio stops, Android kills the background app. On reopen, the page reloads paused at the saved position, which is expected behavior.
   - **Fixes added:** download the next episode ahead of time, retry play once the audio can play, and on an error resume at the same position from the saved copy or the network.

   Generate short voice clips with the ElevenLabs connector (account 761d98d3) for episodes 08, 09 and 10, and download them as MP3s. Do nothing else: no code edits and no commits.
   - Input per episode: D:\project\learn-english\spikes\episode-01\audio\beginner2\epNN\clips.json, a list of {id, who, voice, say}. `say` is sent as-is, including the leading [casual] tag. `voice` is the voice_id.
   - Output: D:\project\learn-english\spikes\episode-01\audio\beginner2\epNN\clips\<id>.mp3. Never overwrite an existing file.
   - Generation: creative_generate_speech with model_id "eleven_v4", generations_count 1, flow_id "cWcb3B1JPfKhKRc6MW0m", voice_id from the clip, prompt = `say`. One call per clip, sent in parallel.
   - **NEVER call generation twice for the same clip to retry (costs credits). If one fails, report it instead.**
   - Poll creative_get_flow_run_status with a few session ids per call. Download each content URL promptly with curl (URLs expire about 2 h after generation; current links show Expires=1794218400).
   - Verify every file exists and is over 5 KB, and check duration with `ffprobe -v error -show_entries format=duration -of csv=p=0 <file>`. Short English lines should be about 0.7–3 s; Korean narrator lines about 1–25 s. Flag anything wrong.
   - Report briefly per episode: saved/expected, failures, suspicious clips (id, text, duration), and credits used.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - The ElevenLabs creative MCP tools return a session_id per generation. The content URL contains `/content_generation/<session_id>/<gen_id>/content.mp3`, which is how each file is mapped back to its clip id.
   - Each episode has 31 clips: n01–n19 (narrator, voice aiUUgjHa4mpHf6UenZuf) and e01–e12 (English voices 7EzWGsX10sAS4c9m9cPf, OYTbf65OHHFELVut7v2H, bIHbv24MWmeRgasZH58o, ZIlrSGI4jZqobxRKprJz).
   - CBR MP3, so identical durations give identical file sizes. That is expected, not duplication.

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - Scratchpad: E:/Temp/claude/12f846cde673f19c/bf843c51-9696-44fd-991b-2d0b72ad91c4/scratchpad
     - ep08.map and ep09.map: lines of "<clip_id> <session_id>".
     - dl.sh: the download helper, final version:
```bash
#!/bin/bash
S="E:/Temp/claude/12f846cde673f19c/bf843c51-9696-44fd-991b-2d0b72ad91c4/scratchpad"
EP=$1; shift
D="/d/project/learn-english/spikes/episode-01/audio/beginner2/ep$EP/clips"
for u in "$@"; do
  sid=$(echo "$u" | sed -E 's#.*/content_generation/([^/]+)/.*#\1#')
  id=$(awk -v s="$sid" '$2==s{print $1}' "$S/ep$EP.map")
  if [ -z "$id" ]; then echo "NOMAP $sid"; continue; fi
  if [ -e "$D/$id.mp3" ]; then echo "EXISTS $id"; continue; fi
  for t in 1 2 3; do
    tmp="$S/tmp_$EP_$id.mp3"; rm -f "$tmp"
    if curl -sS -o "$tmp" "$u"; then mv -n "$tmp" "$D/$id.mp3"; echo "ok $id $(stat -c %s "$D/$id.mp3")"; break; fi
    echo "retry $id";
  done
done
true
```

     - `VOICE`, `DEFAULT` (all "[casual]"), `TAG` (episode 1 emotion tags), `SAY = {"R&B": "R and B"}` (spoken replacement), and the gap constants.
     - `items(e)` yields `(section key, line, clip id n01../e01.., kind)` where kind is line/prompt/answer/pause.
     - `clips(n)` writes `epNN/clips.json` as `[{id, who, voice, say}]` and exits on symbols `~/=+*#&%@_<>|` after the SAY replacement.
     - `build(n)` concatenates and writes `audio/beginner2/epNN/b1-NN-v2.mp3`, `app/b1-NN-v2.webm` and `epNN/timeline.json`.
       - `sound(path, extra="", trim=True)` uses a silenceremove trim when `trim` is true; otherwise it applies `extra` (or `anull`).

2. **Key Technical Concepts**
   - **Gemini TTS via AI Studio** (`GEMINI_API_KEY`, key starts with `AQ.`):
     - The user's project has prepay billing, so there is no free tier. That usage cost real money: about 59 requests, roughly $0.15.
     - Models available: gemini-2.5-flash-preview-tts, gemini-2.5-pro-preview-tts, gemini-3.1-flash-tts-preview, gemini-3.8-flash-tts, gemini-3.8-flash-lite-tts.
     - Endpoint: `generateContent` with `responseModalities:["AUDIO"]` and `speechConfig.voiceConfig.prebuiltVoiceConfig.voiceName`.
     - **The 3.8 model returns `audio/wav` with a header.** Reading it as raw s16le PCM caused a 0.12 s crackle at the end of every clip.
     - The prompt must separate the director's notes from the transcript (`### DIRECTOR'S NOTES ... #### TRANSCRIPT`). Otherwise it reads the instruction aloud.
   - **Google Cloud Text-to-Speech** (`GOOGLE_TTS_API_KEY`, starts with `AIza`, restricted to the TTS API, project "My First Project", id project-34a9e27c-a3ab-49a2-8e4, number 926725340816):
     - Endpoint: `POST https://texttospeech.googleapis.com/v1/text:synthesize` with header `x-goog-api-key`.
     - Body: `{"input":{"text":...},"voice":{"languageCode":"ko-KR"|"en-US","name":"ko-KR-Chirp3-HD-<Voice>"},"audioConfig":{"audioEncoding":"MP3","sampleRateHertz":44100}}`. The response has base64 `audioContent`.
     - Korean voices: 41 in total (Chirp3 30, Neural2 3, Standard 4, Wavenet 4).
     - Chirp3 Korean female voices: Achernar, Aoede, Autonoe, Callirrhoe, Despina, Erinome, Gacrux, Kore, Laomedeia, Leda, Pulcherrima, Sulafat, Vindemiatrix, Zephyr.
     - Chirp 3 HD free tier is about 1M characters per month. It doesn't accept emotion or style prompts. Accent can change by locale (en-GB, en-AU, en-IN), and speed and pauses are adjustable.
     - Gemini models through Cloud TTS: `gemini-3.8-flash-tts` returned "not supported". `gemini-2.5-flash-tts` needs the Agent Platform API (aiplatform) enabled. **Not enabled; the user forbade it.**
   - **App track mechanism:**
     - Listening history is keyed by the index in SCRIPTS/EPS. Comparison tracks (`alt`) are therefore appended at the end of the data and only displayed below episode 1.
     - They're excluded from album counts, and `sibs()` is sorted so next and previous follow the display order.
   - **Publishing:**
     - Artifact: publish `app/episode-01.html` with a `files` map using an absolute `from` path.
     - Vercel: run `build_pwa.py`, then `npx vercel deploy --prod --yes` from `spikes/episode-01/web`. The first deploy sometimes doesn't pick up a new file; redeploy and check with curl.
   - **Preview:** story-pwa-2 on port 8771 serves `spikes/episode-01/web`. To clear the service worker, run JS on the correct tabId; I once ran it on the user's AI Studio tab by mistake.

3. **Files and Code Sections**

   **AI Studio Gemini API**
   - Endpoint: `POST https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-3.8-flash-tts:generateContent`, header `x-goog-api-key`.
   - Single speaker: put the prompt as `### DIRECTOR'S NOTES\n{notes}\nRead only the transcript below, nothing else.\n\n#### TRANSCRIPT\n{text}`, with `speechConfig.voiceConfig.prebuiltVoiceConfig.voiceName`.
   - The response is `audio/wav`.
   - Long "You are …" notes caused the model to read the instructions aloud (28 s clip); shorter notes fixed it.
   - Multi-speaker on 3.8:
     - Each part must be `{"text":…, "speechMetadata":{"speaker":S}}`, plus `multiSpeakerVoiceConfig.speakerVoiceConfigs:[{"speaker":k,"voiceConfig":{"prebuiltVoiceConfig":{"voiceName":v}}}]`.
     - `systemInstruction` is rejected ("Developer instruction is not enabled for this model"), so there is no style note in multi-speaker mode.

   **Cloud Text-to-Speech, Gemini models**
   - Endpoint: `POST https://texttospeech.googleapis.com/v1/text:synthesize`.
   - Auth: `Authorization: Bearer <ADC token>` and `x-goog-user-project: project-34a9e27c-a3ab-49a2-8e4`. The API key gets 403 `aiplatform.endpoints.predict`.
   - Body: `input:{prompt, text}`, `voice:{languageCode:"en-US", name:"Aoede", modelName:"gemini-2.5-flash-tts"}`, `audioConfig:{audioEncoding:"MP3", sampleRateHertz:44100, speakingRate?}`.
   - Multi-speaker: `voice.multiSpeakerVoiceConfig.speakerVoiceConfigs:[{"speakerAlias":k,"speakerId":v}]` with text `"Name: line\n…"`.
   - gemini-3.8 is not supported via Cloud.

   **gcloud**
   - Installed via `winget install --id Google.CloudSDK`.
   - Path: `C:\Users\sky08\AppData\Local\Google\Cloud SDK\google-cloud-sdk\bin\gcloud.cmd`.
   - The user ran `auth application-default login` as xxx.beom.

   **Free Chirp 3 HD**

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Beginner format (memory beginner-expression-format):
     - Sequence: 도입 → 예문 8 (English line + short Korean narrator note) → 정리 → 말해보기 8 (Korean → 3s pause → English) → 끝인사.
     - Voices 남1/여1/남2/여2.
   - Script criteria agreed in discussion:
     - Goal: understand easy everyday native speech.
     - Real current spoken English; no textbook-only phrases; no trendy slang in 초급.
     - 3~6 words.
     - Verify phrases are really said that way, e.g. "I don't have money" → "I don't have any money".
     - Interests: 2~3 per episode, never forced.
     - Narration: Korean 존댓말, short, single-quote citations ('돈 없는'이라는 뜻). Point out words whose meaning differs from the textbook meaning (broke = 돈 없는).
     - Contractions: formal spelling in 초급, mention gonna in the relevant episode.
     - Frequency check: Longman S1~S2, YouGlish, COCA if unsure; recency via recent videos and posts; mark "확인 못 함".
     - Process: 1화 first, 성범 reviews, then the other 24.
     - Existing "1화에서 배운 표현입니다" reminder rule: I recommended keeping it.
   - ElevenLabs MCP connectors:
     - `mcp__67a9ac48-...` has only 6 credits left. Do not use.
     - `mcp__761d98d3-...` works (voice/speech connector). Use this one.
     - Tool flow: `creative_generate_speech` (eleven_v4, voice_id, generations_count 1, flow_id; supports estimate_only) → `creative_get_flow_run_status` → download content_url with curl.
     - Cost was about 1 credit per character of the prompt.
   - Gemini 3.8 TTS:
     - Prompt has a "### DIRECTOR'S NOTES" section plus "#### TRANSCRIPT".
     - Response is WAV; ffmpeg trims silence and applies loudnorm.
     - Free tier has per-minute and daily limits; the daily reset is believed to be midnight Pacific, about 17:00 KST.
     - Linking the Cloud trial billing account means upgrading to paid, and trial credits exclude the Gemini API.
   - Assembly is `spikes/episode-01/beginner_v2.py`:
     - `clips N` writes `audio/beginner2/epNN/clips.json`.
     - `build N [var]` concatenates clips with gaps and sfx, then outputs `app/b1-NN-v2{var}.webm` (Opus 24k) and `timeline.json`.
     - Clip ids are assigned in order: `n##` for narrator lines, `e##` for unique English lines (same voice + same text → one id).
     - `TAG` maps lines to emotion tags (e.g. "I'm late." → [worried]).

       - BASE = home/album/settings. SHEETS = more/timer/queue/size.
       - Going from one sheet to another replaces the top entry.

     - **Back button handler:**
     ```js
     let exitAsk = 0;
     if (CAP && Capacitor.isPluginAvailable('App')) CAP.nativeCallback('App', 'addListener', { eventName: 'backButton' }, () => {
       if (stack.length > 1) return back();
       if (!a.paused) return CAP.nativePromise('App', 'minimizeApp', {}).catch(() => {});
       if (Date.now() - exitAsk < 2600) return CAP.nativePromise('App', 'exitApp', {}).catch(() => {});
       exitAsk = Date.now(); toast('Press back again to exit.');
     });
     ```

       - `#albumDl { margin: 0 -12px 0 auto }`
       - `.dl-ring`: 24px, rotated -90deg, track white 20%, `.p` stroke `var(--hi)` with dasharray 100. `.ing` hides `.lucide` and shows the ring.
     - **Download JS** (`let dl = null; const dlDemo = new Set();`):
       - `showRing()` sets the dashoffset and aria-label.
       - `albumDownload()` steps the ring every 220ms by 3–10%. Tapping again cancels with the toast 'Download canceled.'
       - When the ring is full, after 450ms the button hides and the toast 'Demo only: album files are not on the server yet.' shows.
     - **renderAlbum:** the button is hidden if `!made || all album tracks in S.dled || dlDemo.has(albumSel)`, then `showRing()` runs.

     - **Size sheet:** `<div class="sh-list" id="sizeList"></div>`.
       - `renderSize()` builds `.opt` rows with `data-size` and a check on the selected row, then calls `icons()`.
       - Click handler: `[data-size]` calls setSize, then closeSheets.
       - The slider CSS and JS were removed.
     - **Groups:**
       - `GROUP_EN` maps Korean TOC group names to English.

       - `.trk-h`: caption, uppercase, muted, padding `28px 0 6px`, first-child top 8.
     - **Korean titles:**

       - Rows render `<small>` under the title when `heads` is set.

       - `.trk-go`: `min-height: 44px; padding: 6px 0` (was height 52, then 44).
     - **trackRow(i, n, heads):** podcasts show `` `EP${n}: ${e.title}` `` (via `isPod(e)`).
     - **Toast CSS:**
       - `.toast`: `top: calc(var(--top) + 30px); transform: translate(-50%, calc(-50% - 12px))`
       - `.toast.on`: `transform: translate(-50%, -50%)`
     - **Mini bar markup:** `#miniOpen` wraps `<span class="mini-slide" id="miniSlide">`. `.mini-open` has `overflow:hidden; touch-action:pan-y`.
     - **Mini bar swipe** (IIFE before `$('prev').onclick`):
       - Direction is locked after 8px of movement. A drag past the ends gets 1/3 resistance.
       - It advances if the drag passes 30% of the width or the velocity is over 0.5 px/ms. Otherwise it snaps back in 250ms.
       - To advance it slides out in 180ms with opacity 0, calls `step(dir)`, comes in from the opposite side over 260ms, and is guarded by `busy`.
       - `preventDefault` on touchend stops the click.
   - **`spikes/episode-01/build_pwa.py`:** the SW `audio()` now uses only the cached copy (`audio-v1`). Otherwise it returns `fetch(r)` and no longer caches on play. The header comment was updated.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Lesson format:**
     - 도입 → 예문 8 → 정리 → 말해보기 8 (Korean prompt, (멈춤 3초), `화자: English || 한국어`) → 끝인사.
     - 심화 and 고급 files have extra `### 끝인사` and `### 검수 메모` sections that 초급 doesn't have.
   - **Voices (ElevenLabs eleven_v4, connector 761d98d3 only):** 해설자 Mina `aiUUgjHa4mpHf6UenZuf`, 남1 `7EzWGsX10sAS4c9m9cPf`, 여1 `OYTbf65OHHFELVut7v2H`, 남2 `bIHbv24MWmeRgasZH58o`, 여2 `ZIlrSGI4jZqobxRKprJz`. Default tag `[casual]`.
   - **Cost:** about 1 credit per character; 69 credits ≈ 1.25¢. Never retry a generation call.
   - **Pipeline:**
     - `beginner_v2.py`: `clips N` writes clips.json; `build N` concatenates with gaps (GAP 0.4, AFTER_PAIR 0.8, SECTION_GAP 1.0, PAUSE 3.0), the start/end sfx at -9dB, and the click `sfx-next-soft.mp3`. Then loudnorm → mp3 → 24k opus webm, plus timeline.json.
     - App: `build_page.py` injects TIMELINE and SCRIPTS into `app/template.html` → `app/episode-01.html`.
     - `build_pwa.py` → `web/`. `side-a`: `npm run web` copies web → `side-a/www`. Capacitor appId `com.seongbeom.sidea`, webDir `www`.
     - Phone install: `adb install -r app-release.apk` over wireless debugging (Android plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-09-side-a-android-app.md`).
   - **Template:**
     - `TOC` has albums 초급/심화/고급 with pattern groups. 고급 still has the group '아직 정하지 않음'.
     - `COLOR = {초급:['#031253','b1'], 심화:['#0224d1','b2'], 고급:['#06411b','b3']}`, plus `KO_TITLE`.
     - Album tracks are matched by `SCRIPTS[i].course === al.name && e.n === n`.
     - `has(i)` presumably checks that audio exists. madeIn/doneIn count by course.
     - Saved listening records are keyed by index position, so new entries must be appended at the end of eps/tl to avoid shifting.

1. Primary Request and Intent:
   Generate ElevenLabs speech clips for the 심화 lessons, episodes 14, 15 and 16. The user approved about 1,750 credits per episode.
   - Input for each episode NN: `D:\project\learn-english\spikes\episode-01\audio\intermediate\epNN\clips.json`, a list of 31 items, each `{id, who, voice, say}`.

   - Poll creative_get_flow_run_status until each clip is done, then curl each audio URL into clips/<id>.mp3. Links expire after about 2 hours, so finish each episode's downloads before starting the next episode.
   - HARD RULE: "never call creative_generate_speech twice for the same item, not even to retry. Every call is charged. If an item fails, record it and move on."
   - Check every file with ffprobe; its duration must be more than 0. Map each session_id to its clip id so every file holds the right sentence.

   - Final reply must be under 80 words: per episode, how many clips were saved, any failed ids, and the credits used.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - ElevenLabs creative flows on connector 761d98d3.
   - Each content_url contains `/content_generation/<session_id>/`. The download script asserts this to confirm the id↔session mapping.
   - The queue limit causes 429 errors when about 15 or more generations are queued. Sending batches of about 10 and polling until each batch completes avoids this.
   - Poll outputs are very large, so URLs go into mapfiles using shell variables:
     - P="https://elevenlabsusercontent.com/database/workspace/73061a6b460a4919994ef1cb046eb4d9/content_generation"
     - Q="content.mp3?Expires=1794250800&KeyName=assets-cdn-key-1&Signature"
     - Line format: `id sid $P/sid/genid/$Q=SIG`

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - Download script at `E:/Temp/claude/12f846cde673f19c/c746a846-390f-44b9-a261-a4d6c9c43e7d/scratchpad/adv141516/dl3.py`. It lives in its own folder because a sibling process overwrote scratchpad/dl.py.
     ```python
     import sys, os, subprocess
     ep, mapfile = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
     out = rf"D:\project\learn-english\spikes\episode-01\audio\intermediate\ep{ep}\clips"
     os.makedirs(out, exist_ok=True)
     for line in open(mapfile, encoding="utf-8"):
         parts = line.split()
         if len(parts) != 3: continue
         cid, sid, url = parts
         assert f"/content_generation/{sid}/" in url, (cid, sid)
         dst = os.path.join(out, cid + ".mp3")
         if os.path.exists(dst) and os.path.getsize(dst) > 0:
             print(cid, "exists"); continue
         subprocess.run(["curl", "-sSfL", "-o", dst, url], check=False)
         d = subprocess.run(["ffprobe","-v","error","-show_entries","format=duration","-of","csv=p=0",dst],capture_output=True,text=True).stdout.strip()
         print(cid, d)
     ```
     Run it with `python -I "$S/dl3.py" 16 "$S/ep16a.txt"`, where S is the adv141516 folder.

     #!/bin/bash
     dir="$1"
     while read -r id sid url; do
       [ -z "$id" ] && continue
       case "$url" in *"/$sid/"*) ;; *) echo "MISMATCH $id $sid"; continue;; esac

       echo "$id $d"

For each episode NN, the input is D:\project\learn-english\spikes\episode-01\audio\intermediate\epNN\clips.json, a list of 31 items {id, who, voice, say}. The output is one MP3 per item at ...\epNN\clips\<id>.mp3. Skip any item whose mp3 already exists with duration > 0. Rules:
   - Use ONLY connector 761d98d3 (the other has no credits).
   - Create one flow per episode and pass its flow_id to every generate call.
   - Call creative_generate_speech with prompt = item.say exactly (including the [casual] tag), voice_id = item.voice, model_id "eleven_v4", generations_count 1, and a short context.
   - Poll creative_get_flow_run_status, then download with curl right away. Links expire in about 2 hours, so finish each episode's downloads before starting the next episode.
   - HARD RULE: never call creative_generate_speech twice for the same item, not even to retry. If an item fails, record it and move on.
   - Check each file with ffprobe (duration > 0) and make sure each id gets the matching file. Keep a map from session_id to clip id.
   - If credits are exhausted, stop immediately and report.
   - Don't touch any other files.
   - Final reply in under 80 words: clips saved per episode, failed ids, and credits used.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - ElevenLabs MCP tools on connector 761d98d3: creative_create_flow, creative_generate_speech, creative_get_flow_run_status (at most 20 session_ids per call).
   - The content URL contains `/content_generation/<session_id>/<generation_id>/content.mp3?...Signature=...`. The download script reads the session id from the URL and looks it up in a map file to get the clip id.
   - The queue limit returns a 429 "too many content generations queued". Sending batches of about 6–8 and polling between batches avoids it.
   - The scratchpad is shared with other parallel agents, so I use the private folder `int111213`.

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - Private folder P = E:/Temp/claude/12f846cde673f19c/c746a846-390f-44b9-a261-a4d6c9c43e7d/scratchpad/int111213
   - $P/dl.sh (usage: `bash "$P/dl.sh" EPDIR MAPFILE URLFILE`):
     ```
     #!/bin/bash
     EP="$1"; MAP="$2"; URLS="$3"
     mkdir -p "$EP/clips"
     while read -r url; do
       [ -z "$url" ] && continue
       sid=$(echo "$url" | sed -E 's#.*/content_generation/([^/]+)/.*#\1#')
       id=$(awk -v s="$sid" '$2==s{print $1}' "$MAP")
       if [ -z "$id" ]; then echo "NO MAP for $sid"; continue; fi
       out="$EP/clips/$id.mp3"
       if [ -s "$out" ]; then echo "skip $id"; continue; fi
       curl -sS -f -o "$out" "$url" && echo "ok $id" || echo "FAIL $id"
     done < "$URLS"
     ```
   - Map files use the format "id session_id credits": $P/ep11_map.txt, $P/ep12_map.txt, $P/ep13_map.txt.
   - Verify command: `cd $EP/clips; for f in *.mp3; do printf "%s=%s " $f $(ffprobe -v error -show_entries format=duration -of csv=p=0 $f); done; ls | wc -l`

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - `.../scratchpad/i232425/dl.sh` (private; usage: `dl.sh map outdir urlsfile`):
     ```
     #!/bin/bash
     map="$1"; out="$2"; urls="$3"
     mkdir -p "$out"
     while read -r u; do
       [ -z "$u" ] && continue
       sid=$(echo "$u" | sed -E 's#.*/content_generation/([^/]+)/.*#\1#')
       id=$(awk -v s="$sid" '$2==s{print $1}' "$map")
       if [ -z "$id" ]; then echo "NOMAP $sid"; continue; fi
       if [ -s "$out/$id.mp3" ]; then echo "SKIP $id"; continue; fi
       curl -sSL --connect-timeout 20 --max-time 60 --retry 3 -o "$out/$id.mp3" "$u" && echo "OK $id" || echo "FAIL $id"
     done < "$urls"
     ```

Generate ElevenLabs speech clips for 심화 episodes 8, 9 and 10. The user approved about 1,750 credits per episode. For each NN in 08/09/10:

Steps:

"If the tool says credits are exhausted, stop immediately and report." "Don't touch any other files." Final reply must be under 80 words: per episode, how many clips were saved, any failed ids, and the credits used if the tool reports them.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
- ElevenLabs MCP connector 761d98d3 tools: creative_create_flow, creative_generate_speech, creative_get_flow_run_status, creative_get_flow.
- Each generate call returns a session_id. Content URLs look like `https://elevenlabsusercontent.com/database/workspace/73061a6b460a4919994ef1cb046eb4d9/content_generation/<session>/<gen_id>/content.mp3?Expires=1794250800&KeyName=assets-cdn-key-1&Signature=<sig>`.
- About 10 queued generations triggers a 429. Sending batches of at most 8 and waiting about 25 seconds (`python -c "import time;time.sleep(25)"`) has avoided it since.
- Printing Korean text needs `PYTHONIOENCODING=utf-8`.

3. Files and Code Sections:
- Scratchpad: `E:/Temp/claude/12f846cde673f19c/c746a846-390f-44b9-a261-a4d6c9c43e7d/scratchpad/`
  - `dl.py` takes ep, mapfile and urlfile. For each "id url" line it checks that the session in the URL matches the map entry, skips files that already exist, downloads with curl, and prints the ffprobe duration:
```python
import sys, subprocess, os, json
ep, mapf, urlf = sys.argv[1:4]
base = f"D:/project/learn-english/spikes/episode-01/audio/intermediate/{ep}/clips"
os.makedirs(base, exist_ok=True)
m = {}
for l in open(mapf):
    p = l.split()
    if len(p) >= 2: m[p[0]] = p[1]
for l in open(urlf):
    p = l.split()
    if len(p) != 2: continue
    cid, url = p
    sess = url.split('/content_generation/')[1].split('/')[0]
    if m.get(cid) != sess:
        print("MISMATCH", cid, sess, m.get(cid)); continue
    out = f"{base}/{cid}.mp3"
    if os.path.exists(out) and os.path.getsize(out) > 0:
        print("exists", cid); continue
    r = subprocess.run(["curl", "-sS", "-f", "-o", out, url])
    d = subprocess.run(["ffprobe","-v","error","-show_entries","format=duration","-of","csv=p=0",out],capture_output=True,text=True).stdout.strip()
    print(cid, r.returncode, d)
```
  - Map files `ep08_map.txt`, `ep09_map.txt` and `ep10_map.txt` hold lines of "id session credits". URL files are `epNN_urlsK.txt`.
  - Usage pattern: `P=".../content_generation"; Q="content.mp3?Expires=1794250800&KeyName=assets-cdn-key-1&Signature"`, write lines `id $P/<session>/<gen>/$Q=<sig>`, then `cd "$S" && python -I dl.py epNN epNN_map.txt epNN_urlsK.txt`.

<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
Generate ElevenLabs speech clips for the 고급 (advanced) episodes 8, 9, 11 and 12, in that order. The user approved the spend (about 32,000 credits across all helpers) and said: if credits run out, stop and leave the rest.
- Input: `D:\project\learn-english\spikes\episode-01\audio\advanced\epNN\clips.json`, a list of {id, who, voice, say}. Read it with Python using utf-8 and PYTHONIOENCODING=utf-8, because the console is cp949.
- Output: `...\epNN\clips\<id>.mp3`. Skip any item whose mp3 already exists with duration > 0.
- Reuse first: copy a matching clip (same voice and identical say) from any other advanced/intermediate folder.
- Tools: use ONLY connector 761d98d3: creative_create_flow, creative_generate_speech and creative_get_flow_run_status.
  - Create one flow per episode.
  - Call creative_generate_speech with prompt = say exactly (including the [casual] tag), voice_id = voice, model_id "eleven_v4", generations_count 1 and a short context.
  - Send at most 4 calls per message, and wait for them to finish before sending more.
  - Poll, then download with curl right away. The links expire after about 2 hours, so finish each episode before starting the next.
- HARD RULE: never call generate twice for the same item if the first call returned a session_id. The one exception is a 429 "too many generations queued" with no session_id: wait about 20 seconds and retry once. If it is refused again, record it and move on.
- If refused with daily_generation_limit_reached, or if credits are insufficient: stop immediately and report.
- Check every file with ffprobe, and keep a map from session_id to clip id.
- Don't touch any other files.
- Final reply under 80 words: per episode, clips saved / total, missing ids and why, credits used, and whether a limit was hit.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
- ElevenLabs flow/session/generation IDs
- Content URL pattern: `https://elevenlabsusercontent.com/database/workspace/73061a6b460a4919994ef1cb046eb4d9/content_generation/<session>/<genid>/content.mp3?Expires=1794283200&KeyName=assets-cdn-key-1&Signature=<sig>`

  - Builds a reuse index. It found no reusable clips.
  - Its argv handling gave odd output; don't rely on it.
- `map.txt`

- `dl.sh EP ID URL`
  - Checks that the URL contains the mapped session.
  - Curls to `D:/project/learn-english/spikes/episode-01/audio/advanced/epEP/clips/ID.mp3`, then prints the duration.
- `d.sh EP ID SESSION GENID SIG`
  - Builds the URL and calls dl.sh.
  - Usage: `D=".../scratchpad/d.sh"; $D 11 n11 <session> <genid> "<sig>"`

         ```
       - `syncSbar()` now calls `if (v?.id === 'vAlbum') fadeCover(v, b);`
       - `fadeCover`:
         ```
         function fadeCover(v, b) {
           const box = $('albumCover'), cv = box.querySelector('.cv');
           if (!cv) return;
           const bottom = box.getBoundingClientRect().bottom - parseFloat(getComputedStyle(box).paddingBottom);
           const range = bottom + v.scrollTop - b.offsetHeight, vis = range > 0 ? Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (bottom - b.offsetHeight) / range)) : 1;
           box.style.setProperty('--cvo', (vis * vis).toFixed(3));
           box.style.setProperty('--cvs', (.88 + .12 * vis).toFixed(3));
         }
         ```
       - `renderAlbum` resets the fade: after `$('vAlbum').style.setProperty('--c', al.c);` it runs `$('albumCover').style.removeProperty('--cvo'); $('albumCover').style.removeProperty('--cvs');`
       - The `.sbar.alb` background is now `... no-repeat 0 var(--sy, 0px) / 100% 100000px transparent`. Previously it was `/ 100% 600px, #121212`, and that fallback color caused a dark 1px line at the band's bottom edge.

       - A: I've been waiting in line for ten minutes.
       - B: Well, I'm in a hurry.
       - A: So is everyone. The line starts back there.
       - B: Fine, whatever.
     - **Ep2 (A B A B A B A)**
       - A: Hey, sorry to bother you, but can you keep it down?
       - B: What? We're not that loud.
       - A: It's midnight, and I can hear everything through the wall.
       - B: It's just a few friends. Relax.
       - A: I get it, but I have to work early tomorrow.
       - B: Okay, okay. We'll turn the music down.
       - A: Thanks. I appreciate it.
     - **Ep3 (B start)**
       - B: Hi, how can I help you?
       - A: I was charged twice for the same order.
       - B: Let me check. I only see one charge here.
       - A: Here's my bank app. You can see both charges right here.
       - B: Oh, you're right. I'm sorry about that.
       - A: Can you refund the second one today?
       - B: Yes, I'll process it right now.
     - **Ep4 (A start)**
       - A: This is the third time my order has been wrong.
       - B: I'm sorry, but there's nothing I can do.
       - A: Then I'd like to speak to the manager, please.
       - B: The manager is busy right now.
       - A: That's okay. I can wait.
       - B: Fine. I'll go get her.
       - A: Thank you.
     - **Ep5 (B start)**
       - B: Are you serious? Even a kid could do this right.
       - A: Whoa. Don't talk to me like that.
       - B: I'm just being honest.
       - A: There's a difference between honest and rude.
       - B: Okay, maybe I went too far.
       - A: If there's a problem, just tell me calmly.
       - B: Fair enough. Sorry.
     - **Ep6 (A start)**

       - B: Same thing, isn't it?
       - A: Not at all. Please don't put words in my mouth.
       - B: Okay, sorry. I misunderstood.
       - A: It's fine. Let's just be clear.
     - **Ep7 (B start)**

       - A: Seriously? You always do this.
       - B: That's not fair. Something came up.
       - A: Something always comes up. I cancelled my plans for this.
       - B: I said I was sorry.
       - A: Sorry isn't enough anymore.
       - B: Okay. I get why you're upset.
     - **Ep8 (A start)**

       - A: Okay, maybe I did. Can we both calm down?
       - B: Fine. I'm just really stressed.
       - A: I know. Let's talk about it later, when we're not so angry.
       - B: Yeah. That's probably better.
       - A: Okay.
     - **Ep9 (A start)**

       - B: Sure. What's up?
       - A: Honestly, I was hurt by what you said.
       - B: Really? I didn't mean it like that.

       - B: Thanks for telling me. I'm sorry.
       - A: Thanks. That means a lot.

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`content/stories/words-01.md` (new)**
     - Header `## 1화 Morning Routine`, then sections 도입, 단어, 복습, 끝인사.
     - Row format: `단어 | 단어 목소리 | 뜻(복습 질문) | 해설 | 예문 목소리 | 예문 || 예문 뜻 | 마지막 줄`
     - Irregular rows use "과거형은 X라고 해요." / "과거형은 X이라고 해요."
     - The forget row: `forget | 여2 | 잊다 | '잊다'라는 뜻이에요. 과거형은 forgot이라고 해요. | 남2 | I forgot my phone at home. || 폰을 집에 두고 왔어요. | forget, forgot`

   - **`spikes/episode-01/build_words.py` (new)**
     - Constants:
       ```
       GAP, WORD_GAP, PAUSE = 0.4, 2.0, 2.0
       REPEAT, REPEAT_GAP = 3, 1.2
       ```
     - `script(n)` returns `{n, title, intro, review, outro, words[]}`.
     - `steps(s)` yields, per word:
       - `("words", wv, word, None, "word")`
       - `("words", "해설자", note, None, "line")`
       - `("words", ev, ex, None, "line")`
       - `("words", "해설자", exko, None, "line")`
       - `("words", wv, last, None, "repeat")`
     - The review yields: prompt (narrator Korean), pause, then `answer` with meaning None.
     - `ids(s)` loads the existing clips.json mapping (who, say without tag) → id, so ids stay stable, and new lines get the next numbers.
     - `clips(n)` writes clips.json.
     - `build(n)`:
       - Intro `sound(RAW / "sfx-start-words.mp3", cut + ",volume=-6dB")`.
       - Trims each clip with `cut = "silenceremove=start_periods=1:start_threshold=-45dB,areverse,silenceremove=start_periods=1:start_threshold=-45dB,areverse"`.
       - The repeat plays 3 times with 1.2s silence between.
       - Click sound `sfx-next-soft.mp3` between review items.
       - End sound `sfx-end.mp3`.
       - Concats wav parts, applies loudnorm, writes `w1-NN.mp3`, then `w1-NN.webm` (libopus 24k), then `timeline.json`.
       - Lyric kind is `"line" if kind == "repeat" else kind`, so `word` stays a kind and is used for the CSS gap.

   - **`spikes/episode-01/build_page.py`**
     - Added after the shadowing loop, before the drafts:
       ```
       from build_words import script as words_script
       for d in sorted((HERE / "audio" / "words").glob("ep[0-9][0-9]")):
           ...eps.append({"n": n, "title": words_script(n)["title"], ..., "course": "단어 1"})
           f = f"w1-{n:02d}.webm"; tl.append(timeline | {"file": f}); shutil.copy(d / f, HERE / "app" / f)
       ```

   - **`spikes/episode-01/app/template.html`**
     - New album list:
       ```
       const WORDS = [{ name: '단어 1', en: 'Words 1', mark: 'W1', about: 'The everyday words you hear most, 20 at a time', c: '#1d5c63', pats: ['Morning Routine'] }];
       ```
     - ALBUMS adds `...WORDS.map(p => ({ ...p, pod: true, words: true }))`.
     - SHELVES adds `['Words', ALBUMS.filter(a => a.words)]`.
     - The Podcasts filter excludes words. The EP prefix excludes words.
     - KO_TITLE adds `"Morning Routine": "아침 일과"`.
     - CSS: `.ln.word:not(.gap) { margin-top: 56px }`. The border line was removed.

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **Couldn't find the morning discussion:** I first said the discussion wasn't recorded. It was in this session's own transcript (a python grep of the jsonl found it).
   - **Google free-tier limit:** hit the 429 limit of 10 requests per day. The third line was substituted.
   - **Cost estimate too low:** estimated about 1,300 credits; the actual was 2,041 because of the per-clip minimum.
   - **Clip ids would shift when inserting narrator lines:** ids() now loads the existing clips.json to keep ids stable.
   - **Lyrics divider:** I added a border line, but the user only wanted space. Removed it and kept a 56px margin.
   - **"Korean pronunciation" of forgot:** I regenerated n69 when changing to 과거형. The user reports it still says "포가트". Not fixed yet.
